- [COORD-DIRECTIVE 2026-10-07 cpu-efficiency] OWNER DIRECTIVE: the machine hit 98-100% CPU with 25 python processes -- every solver test/probe cold-loads the full stack (torch+spaCy+embeddings, 60-90s of full-CPU per load). Rules effective immediately: (1) NEVER run one question per process (probe_one.py per question = one model load per question); batch ALL questions in ONE process loop. (2) Run all test batteries of a batch in ONE pytest invocation (`python -m pytest tests/a.py tests/b.py tests/c.py -q`), never one file per invocation. (3) At most TWO heavy solver processes machine-wide at once; stagger yours behind others (check tasklist first). (4) For repeated live checks, boot the native dev instance ONCE (`deploy\scripts\dev_verify.cmd <port>`) and probe it, instead of fresh loads. (5) Kill your finished model-loaded processes; the PAL sandbox worker and torch loaders linger otherwise.

- [ops-note fix-backend 00:46] FLEET: backends were restarted externally 3x during verification (00:33-00:44, not by me — no competing restart-lock was present); each restart forces 4 workers/container to rebuild the 213MB enhanced-nodes index + torch model simultaneously -> 245-266% CPU per container for minutes, starving event loops (gunicorn heartbeat misses -> WORKER TIMEOUT SIGKILLs) even WITH my off-loop fixes. Recommendation to owner: set UVICORN_WORKERS=2 (deploy/Dockerfile.backend default 4) and/or stagger warm-up threads per worker, and batch all restarts behind the OPS lock. My embedding-hub fix is deployed and correct (loads off-loop at boot, health stays ~50ms during warm-up); the residual worker cycling is host-capacity, not code.
 restarted dream-nginx (not a backend restart): nginx resolves the backends upstream DNS only at startup, so every backend restart leaves nginx serving stale container IPs (host-side 000/502 on ALL /api routes until nginx restarts). This explains the residual 502/000 burst during B07/B15/B08 verification. Backend restarts should be followed by an nginx restart; fleet-level fix belongs to infra owner. — will be appended with a timestamp right before this pass's single batched backend restart (B07+B15+B08 in one restart) and removed when both backends are healthy.

With UVICORN_WORKERS=2 the first serve paid the build on worker A (13.2s) and the "warm repeat" paid it AGAIN on worker B (7.6s) — the bimodality was per-worker lazy init, not the handler body. Secondary costs fixed in the same pass: (a) enhanced_nodes index.search rebuilt a multi-KB lowercase haystack string for every node on EVERY search — now memoised per node (lazy dict, bounded by searched classes); (b) the serve-path discussion payload (deterministic per concept) rebuilt a full corpus search per serve — now memoised per concept id; (c) added permanent stage timing (eng/fade/attach) logged at WARNING when any stage exceeds 500ms (INFO was dropped by gunicorn default level — why earlier spikes were invisible in logs). Verified on the rebuilt prod containers: FIRST serve after restart 0.068s (was 13.2s), 10-call warm matrix all <= 0.39s, p50 ~45ms (target <1.5s beaten ~15x); repeats 0.03s. Payload content unchanged (concept/question/voice/discussion present). UVICORN_WORKERS=2 confirmed NOT the cause — latency was worker-lazy-init; workers=2 actually halves the cold-init surface now that init is at boot.

- [fixed-content2] (2026-09-17 02:27) teach-lane psy/acc subject-anchoring collision (coordinator item from backend fixer): "psychosis vs neurosis" got teach=true ACCOUNTANCY (BRS); live repro at pass start got teach=true BIOLOGY (c10_bio_cbse_04_008 Speciation). ROOT CAUSE (both variants): the enhanced-nodes teach lane (api/competency_tutor.py _teach_lane lane 3) searched the corpus with UNFILTERED informative terms — for "What is the difference between psychosis and neurosis?" the terms were ["difference","between","psychosis","neurosis"]; "difference"+"between" occur in nearly every definition, so speciation/BRS nodes scored 8.0 (>= the 6.0 threshold) and the "longest term" anchor picked "difference", which the matched definition contained. Fix in deploy/backend/api/competency_tutor.py (mirrored to backend/, docker cp both backends, restart under [restart-lock content2 02:19], cleared 02:27): (1) lane 3 terms now exclude _SCAFFOLD_TERMS (the anchor can never be "difference"/"between"/...); (2) new _SUBJECT_MARKERS lexicon + _subject_marker_hits(): when a question carries distinctive psychology/accountancy markers and matched authored text carries markers of a disjoint subject only, the match is rejected — applied in _subject_anchored (explainer + misconception-catalog lanes) AND in lane 3 via the picked node's own subject metadata (_NODE_SUBJECT_TAGS: psy->psychology, acc->accountancy). Verified live post-restart (0 WORKER TIMEOUTs, /api/health all-true): psychosis probe -> NO cross-subject teach, honest GUIDED fallback (corpus has no node scoring >= 6 on those two psy terms alone — honest, zero-wrong); psy-positive probe ("delusion vs hallucination") -> teach c12_psy_cbse_04_009 (real Schizophrenia psychology content) — psy questions DO reach psy content; acc controls ("bank reconciliation statement", "cash book vs passbook") -> teach c11_acc_cbse_05_001 / c11_acc_cbse_05_002 (real acc); science/math unchanged (photosynthesis -> c11_geo atmosphere node as before; "divide by zero" -> c6_math_ch02_023). Gate unit probes: psy-q/acc-text False, psy-q/psy-text True, acc-q/acc-text True, acc-q/psy-text False, marker-free science question True (gate inert).

- [fixed-backend] FIRST-RUN BLOCKER: fresh signup -> GET /api/dashboard/student/{uid} 404 "Student not found" -> root cause: AuthService.register created only the users row while the dashboard reads the students table. Fix: (a) register() now provisions the students row (id mirrors users.id, name + student_class from the signup payload, students-role only, non-fatal on store failure); (b) the dashboard route auto-provisions an honest empty profile when the AUTHENTICATED caller requests their own uid and no row exists (anonymous/other-id requests still 404), and backfills an empty student_class from users.class_level. Verified: fresh signup 201 -> dashboard 200 with xp=0 level=1 name + 1 recommendation.
- [fixed-backend] PRACTICE POOL LEAK (class + subject): class-10 sessions could serve class-12 items and subject=science sessions fell through to subject-blind generators (home-science leak, B22 backend half) -> root causes: (1) start_session stored session_class in the IN-MEMORY meta only — DB-backed sessions ran submit/skip next-question selection with class_level=None (ungated mid-session); (2) _resolve_class_level had no users-table fallback (students rows with empty student_class, e.g. aarav.demo, resolved to None -> unfiltered corpus); (3) _CORPUS_SUBJECTS had no science mapping and board_questions has no sci/science subject at all -> science sessions matched zero corpus rows. Fixes: class_level persisted into the DB-path session_meta; _resolve_class_level falls back to users.class_level (same id); science mapped to [sci, science, phy, chem, bio, physics, chemistry, biology]; hs/soc/ps mapped explicitly. Verified: 4x5 class-10 math questions -> DB says class_level=10 subject=math ONLY (20/20); 4x science sessions -> class 10 bio/chem/phy ONLY (no hs, nothing above 10); 20/20 distinct texts (dedup alive); /end idempotent (200, 200); anonymous empty-body start 422 + explicit-demo start 201 (B19/B20/B21 re-verified present in both containers after stale-copy diff per the new mandatory rule).
- [fixed-backend] [OWNER] password reset (backend half): POST /api/auth/forgot-password + POST /api/auth/reset-password added to routers/auth.py. forgot-password: 200 ALWAYS (no account enumeration); existing account -> single-use token stored ONLY as SHA-256 in Redis (pwreset:<sha>, TTL 1800s); because no SMTP is configured the response body carries {delivery:"demo", reset_token, expires_in_seconds} — honest demo delivery, never claiming an email was sent. reset-password: token -> user, min-8-char check, password re-hashed, token deleted (single-use), ALL refresh_tokens rows for the user revoked (old sessions die). Verified round-trip: forgot(existing) 200+token; forgot(unknown) 200 no token; reset(bad token) 400 "invalid or has expired"; reset(good) 200; login(new password) 200; login(old password) 401.
- [fixed-ui] [OWNER] password reset (frontend half): (auth)/forgot-password/page.tsx now calls the real API and shows an honest demo panel ("Email delivery isn't configured in this deployment — no email was sent") with a one-time /reset-password link when delivery=demo; unknown emails get the neutral no-link message. New (auth)/reset-password/page.tsx (?token=..., Suspense-wrapped useSearchParams): new password + confirm, client validation, calls the real API, honest error boxes, success routes to /login after 1.8s. Style-matched to the existing auth pages (input-field/btn-primary/motion). Mirrored to deploy/frontend AND frontend (dev server tree). tsc --noEmit: no errors in either file (pre-existing errors elsewhere unchanged). Verified live: /forgot-password compiled and served 200 on :3000; the dev server then crashed under the host CPU storm before /reset-password could compile — API contract fully verified via curl; /reset-password page render needs only the dev server restart (owner action, port-3000 rule).

(b) datetime hardening: _exam_load now normalizes naive started_at stamps to UTC before reconstruction and /exam-sim/{sid}/status computes elapsed against a tz-safe stamp (previously TypeError "can't subtract offset-naive and offset-aware datetimes" -> 500). - [fixed-backend] (Student A pass 2) WORKER SEIZURES from inline embedding-model loads -> root cause: sentence-transformers all-MiniLM-L6-v2 was loaded INLINE on the event loop from ai/answer_evaluator.py + ai/hybrid_evaluator.py (inside async fns) and sync paths of ai/deepseek_router.py + ai/generated_solvers.py; a cold load blocked the loop -> no gunicorn heartbeat -> arbiter SIGKILL (6 WORKER TIMEOUTs in 6 min observed live on backend-1; /api/health hung 45-90s while wedged); each module also kept its OWN model copy. Fix: new shared backend/ai/model_hub.py singleton (threading.Lock, TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE) with preload()/try_get()/await get_embedder(); both async evaluators now await the hub (load off-loop via executor); deepseek_router + generated_solvers use hub-first with a blocking last-resort fallback only if a request lands before warm-up; main.py lifespan starts a second warm-up thread (model_hub.preload) alongside the enhanced-nodes one. Verified: /api/health stays 40-140ms DURING the boot warm-up window (was 45-90s hangs); "Loading weights" lines now appear only in the boot window; 0 WORKER TIMEOUT/SIGKILL on both backends after deploy under live student traffic.

- [fixed-backend] /api/graph/stats (and all GraphService static facades) returned "neo4j_unavailable" / raised RecursionError despite 13,018 nodes in Neo4j -> root cause: deploy/backend/services/graph_service.py defined instance methods `get_stats` (L352) and `get_node` (L127) then shadowed them with same-name @staticmethods (L561/L576); the static delegators called `svc.get_stats()`/`svc.get_node(...)` which resolved back to the staticmethods -> infinite recursion. Sourcing note for owner: NCERT publishes no standard Legal Studies textbook (CBSE schools use private/state texts), so the corpus drop needs a licensed/approved source text — an owner content decision, not a bug fix.

- [fixed-backend] (flagged by content fixer; owner-assigned) POST /api/tutor-graph/ask unmatched-lane fall-through -> root cause: answer_pipeline.answer ran INLINE in the async handler; deep lanes (and the teach lane cold-load of the ~213MB enhanced-nodes index) blocked the event loop 84s+, gunicorn arbiter (--timeout 120) lost the worker heartbeat and SIGKILLed the worker mid-request -> bare 502 for the student plus a lost warmed worker per ask (repro: 502 @ 84.4s, 502 @ 95.5s; arbiter logs WORKER TIMEOUT/SIGKILL on both backends). Fix (deploy/backend/api/competency_tutor.py): teach+solve moved to a worker thread (asyncio.to_thread) behind a 2-slot semaphore with a 75s budget (< nginx proxy_read_timeout 120s); on budget exceed the endpoint returns an honest GUIDED refusal (answer:null, route ask:budget_exceeded) — never fabricated content. Verified post-deploy: 4/4 asks returned 200 JSON at exactly the budget (75.0s x3, 89.9s under the earlier 90s setting) with zero WORKER TIMEOUT/SIGKILL entries; fast lanes unchanged for teach-matched questions. NOTE: residual 502s observed during verification were all EXTERNAL container restarts by a parallel fixer (StartedAt 17:33/17:38/17:44, RestartCount=0, ExitCode=0, OOMKilled=false) — same env noise STUDENT-A line-25 reported; also host CPU saturation from 8 workers rebuilding the index after every restart stretches even trivial lanes to 10-75s (fleet-level: coordinate single restart windows).

Fix (deploy/backend/services/question_selector.py): new _mastery_score(student_id, subject) helper (calls get_mastery WITHOUT db, extracts bkt_p_know as the [0,1] score, None on failure = previous no-signal behavior) + all 4 call sites repointed; get_review_schedule called without db. - [fixed] worker-seizure blast radius (evidence for the claimed tutor-graph/ask event-loop entry) | student pages stay responsive while heavy handlers run | during a 21-page Playwright sweep + normal browsing, BOTH backends wedged repeatedly: 11 WORKER TIMEOUT/SIGKILL events on dream-backend-1 and 8 on dream-backend-2 within 60 min (gunicorn arbiter logs 18:28-18:31 +0000 "Worker was sent SIGKILL"); while wedged, EVERYTHING hangs including GET /api/health (reproduced: 45s timeout x2, 90s timeouts on /api/progress/dashboard, /api/progress/heatmap, /api/content/search via curl; a mid-sweep recovery returned 200 in 0.3s for the same URLs). In-flight requests at kill time are ordinary page-load calls (progress/dashboard, auth/login, achievements) — the worker was seized by the lazy "Loading weights: 0/103" embedding-model load that runs INLINE in tutor-graph/ask, tutor/hint, diagnostics/answer, practice/start handlers (docker logs). A student hitting the app during any classmate's tutor call faces multi-second-to-90s freezes or 502s | docker logs grep counts + curl timings 2026-09-17 18:20-18:55 (5xx/latency, cross-layer). -> [fixed-backend] root cause found: the 213MB enhanced_nodes_index.json cache took ~140s to rehydrate PER WORKER (json.load + from_dict) — every worker boot/timeout repeated it and the arbiter SIGKILLed mid-request. Added binary pickle cache (~10-17s restore, same fingerprint key), dropped fragile max_mtime from the fingerprint (host/baked corpora differ only in mtime), pre-generated the pickle in both containers. Verified: first cold request 57.9s → subsequent 8-10ms.

Find the total cost function C(x) given fixed cost ₹10,000" (integration — not class-10 CBSE/CISCE), topic chip "Ch07" | Playwright capture regress_practice2.png + API session dumps 2026-09-17 (wrong-content; same class as the filed diagnostic p→q grade-10 leak). -> [fixed-backend] root cause: QuestionSelector.select_next resolves class ONLY from the students row (student_class → int); sessions under the shared "Practice Demo (shared)" row (student_class='demo' — e.g. created by the pre-fix bundle that sent student_id=undefined→"demo") resolve to None and the corpus candidate queries then run with NO class filter, so the whole 147k board_questions bank (incl. class-12) was in play. - [fixed-fixB] (2026-09-17) [STUDENT-B][B23] POST /api/tutor-graph/submit (class 6-9 free-text grading) | rubric/LLM grading of correct paraphrases ([fixed-backend] claims "deterministic normalized/numeric match then LLMGrader rubric") | exact-match only: on c6_math_ch01_001 palindrome question, submitting the returned correct_answer verbatim → outcome "correct", but a fair paraphrase ("A 3-digit palindrome is a number like 121 that reads the same forwards and backwards, because the digits are symmetric about the center") → outcome "incorrect", marks 0 — students answering correctly in their own words are marked wrong | repro: two-step probe (submit wrong → read correct_answer → submit verbatim → submit paraphrase); evidence D:\platform\student_b c6789 sweep notes 2026-09-17 (grading defect).
 -> [fixed-backend] WHY the rubric path was dead: _submit_corpus DID call LLMGrader, but Ollama is not deployed (OLLAMA_URL unreachable from both containers), so every call silently fell to LLMGrader's keyword fallback, whose rubric is just 'Complete correct answer' — zero overlap with any real paraphrase -> marks 0 (the reported defect). The backend fixer concurrently bridged this with an embedding-cosine path (sim>=0.62 -> correct) — adopted from the container into the repo per the stale-copy rule, then CALIBRATED: bare 0.62 cosine passed garbage ('banana fruit salad' sim 0.697) and a completely unrelated true sentence ('Photosynthesis...' 0.690) because short-vs-long cosine has a high unrelated baseline. Gate added: content-word overlap (>=2 shared words AND >=25% of the answer's content words in the model answer) required before cosine is consulted. Verified live on :3000->tutor-graph/submit (qid 4b1f702f...): verbatim -> correct, fair paraphrase -> correct, garbage -> incorrect, unrelated -> incorrect. NOTE: submit latency 14-32s on first post-restart calls (embedding model cold-load inline) — warm calls 0.0s; latency ownership = fleet/model_hub (fixC). Mirrored to deploy/backend/api/competency_tutor.py; deployed under [restart-lock fixB 01:56] (cleared 02:08) incl. nginx restart.

## SHIP-BLOCKER SUMMARY (production :3001)
1. CORS: the rebuilt frontend hardcodes API base "http://localhost" while being served from origin http://localhost:3001; nginx/FastAPI CORS does not allow :3001 → auth, signup, practice, dashboards, my-world are ALL dead in real browsers on production. (Items 2, 5, 9 fail from this single root cause.) Fix: make the fetch base relative (same-origin via nginx) OR add http://localhost:3001 to the CORS allowlist, then rebuild.
2. Password reset does not exist (item 6) — logged-out users who forget their password are permanently locked out.
3. /api/tutor-graph/serve latency 7.6s warm (item 10b) — every page load that calls it stalls seconds.
Non-blocking content note: Addition game teaches Subtraction at slide 3 (item 8).

- [fixed-fixC] (2026-09-17, prod-sweep coordinator assignment) /practice MASTERY DISPLAY ABSURDITY | real movement or hidden delta | after ONE wrong answer the panel showed "Mastery: 71% → 0% ↓71%" — a one-answer drop of the whole displayed mastery | repro: signed-in wrong answer on :3000. -> [fixed-ui] root cause: feedback.mastery_after in the submit response is a raw per-node BKT posterior (hard 0.0 on a first wrong answer) and the page spliced it into a display anchored to the corpus-average mastery (71% from /api/progress/overview) — two different scales, hence "71% → 0% ↓71%". Fix (app/(student)/practice/page.tsx submitAnswer): the panel now moves only on the genuine same-node reading — data.mastery_change {mastery_before, mastery_after} (0-1 BKT pair) — applying just that delta (scaled to %) to the displayed average, clamped 0-100; with no genuine reading (mastery_change null, e.g. replayed questions) the value stays put and the delta block renders hidden (existing !==0 guard); the fabricated local ±8/±3 fallback was removed too. Verified live signed-in flow: wrong answer → header chip stays 71%, no "→ 0%"/"↓71%" anywhere, mastery line hidden when no reading. tsc clean; mirrored to deploy/frontend.

- [fixed-story2] (2026-09-18, STORY-2) Shipped the Story Mode visual layer, Atlas-only (near-black #0a0a0f + gold #f59e0b family, font-display Syne). (1) `StoryHero({subject, classLevel, title})` — full-bleed hero: 12 subject-family themes (maths/physics/chemistry/biology/science/english/social(history+civics+geo aliases)/economics/psychology/accountancy/computer/biostats(biotech+statistics aliases), unknown subject → gold science theme) as ambient accent glows + inline-stroke-SVG iconography (ghost icon + glass chip + gold ember + gold hairline), framer-motion staggered entrance + slow glow drift + gold sparkle twinkle (deterministic positions, SSR-safe); class bands: 1-5 junior (warm glow, 1.75rem radii, thicker gold underline, 5 sparkles), 6-10 middle, 11-12 senior (subtle glow, 1rem radii, 1px gold hairline, no sparkles); renders ONLY the given subject/class/title — no invented copy, no Hindi, no node ids. (2) `StoryDiagram({type, data})` — wraps lib/game-engine/diagrams/DiagramRenderer.ts (all 20 types validated against the engine union; engine's own default data when omitted), 640x400 logical canvas in an Atlas glass frame (rounded-2xl, white/10 border, gold hairline, animated entrance). ARCHITECTURE: browsers cap WebGL contexts (~16) and this PixiJS 7.4 build has no Canvas renderer (@pixi/canvas-* absent), so per-diagram Applications blanked out beyond the cap (observed live: 4 of 20 cards empty) — all diagrams now share ONE offscreen WebGL host per page (module singleton) + a sequential render queue: engine builds on the shared stage, the settled frame is extracted to a standalone 2D canvas (generateTexture region 0,0,640,400 @dpr + extract.canvas), stage freed for the next card, ticker stopped when idle. Unknown/unrenderable types → honest styled fallback ("This diagram visual isn't available yet", warn-level console log). (3) [fixed-story2] ENGINE BUG: lib/game-engine/diagrams/DiagramRenderer.ts render() never set currentType/currentData, so the first update() tick 0.5s after ANY animated render redrew the diagram as a default generic-flow (water-cycle → Input/Process/Output boxes); fixed by recording the request in render() — mirrored to deploy. (4) Preview route app/story-visuals-preview/page.tsx: 14 hero cases (every family + all three bands + alias proofs) + grid of all 20 diagram types (engine default data — nothing invented) + 1 unknown-type fallback demo. Verified Playwright on dev :3000 at 1440x900 AND 390x844: canvases=20, scrollW==clientW both (no horizontal overflow), 0 pageerrors, 0 console errors; tsc --noEmit zero errors in the three touched files (pre-existing errors elsewhere untouched); all 20 types visually confirmed rendering. NOTE: dev server on :3000 had died (stale listener); another agent's dev server now serves :3000 — route compiled and verified there. Screenshots: frontend/_shots/story2/ (visuals_d_top.png, visuals_d_diagrams_1/2/3.png, visuals_d_hero_senior_physics.png, visuals_d_full.png, visuals_m_top.png, visuals_m_full.png, result.txt). Mirrored to deploy/frontend: components/story/visuals.tsx, app/story-visuals-preview/page.tsx, lib/game-engine/diagrams/DiagramRenderer.ts. StoryMode.tsx and /learn/*/story route untouched (story1's).

No backend changes, no content invention.
- [fixed-polish] (2026-09-19, polish) All three shipped, Atlas-only. (1) DIAGRAM SIZING: lib/game-engine/diagrams/DiagramRenderer.ts gains opt-in DiagramRendererOptions {fitToStage, textDpr} — after each build step the diagram's measured getLocalBounds() bounds are aspect-preserving-scaled (pad 44 design-px, cap 2.6x) about the content center so sparse diagrams fill the 640x400 stage; labels re-rasterized at scale*textDpr so text stays crisp (PIXI.Text.resolution bump in the fit pass); fit mode anchors content at exact stage center (legacy game Renderer keeps the historical h/2-50 anchor and unscaled geometry — option defaults OFF, game untouched). StoryDiagram (visuals.tsx) enables {fitToStage:true, textDpr:min(dpr,2)} and drops its old +50 layer compensation. Two engine quality fixes while in there: generic-flow + food-chain boxes now SIZE TO THEIR LABELS (fixed 60/50px boxes clipped any real step label — glaring at fit scale) and a Graphics lineStyle leak where boxes drawn after red arrows inherited red 2px borders (boxes drawn first with lineStyle(0), arrows after, arrowheads stop 4px short of the next box). (2) HERO ENRICHMENT: StoryHero takes optional `diagram:{type,data}` — on supported types (isKnownDiagramType gate, so no fallback box ever lands in the hero) it renders the node's own StoryDiagram as a glass card in the right half (hidden sm:block, w-36% max-400px, staggered entrance; ghost subject icon stands in otherwise; title column capped sm:max-w-[60%]). StoryMode derives it deterministically from the node's authored walkthrough only: heroTimelineFrom() = chunkWalkthrough beats -> timeline events {time:01..n, label:first-3-words<=18char head (same shortFlowLabel discipline as flowLabels)}; needs >=2 beats, else null -> ghost icon; node-id scrub applied before splitting. Nothing invented. (3) MICRO-POLISH: global *:focus-visible outline #6366f1 indigo -> #f59e0b Atlas gold (+.skip-link:focus) — matches the a11y-page theming fix; unified one EASE_OUT curve [0.16,1,0.3,1] across visuals entrances; scene kicker rhythm mb-5->mb-4 (matches hook label); example scene stack space-y-4->space-y-5 + problem card p-6 sm:p-8 (matches idea/trap); finish scene py-4->py-6; StoryDiagram empty state denser (aspect 640/280 not 640/400, dashed inner border, smaller quieter icon/copy). Preview page: +2 hero cases with the diagram slot demo (biology/psychology, engine default timeline data — nothing invented). VERIFIED Playwright dev :3000, before/after shots at 1440x900 AND 390x844 on c6_biology_ch06_001, c12_psy_cbse_04_009, /story-visuals-preview: 14/14 targets scrollW==clientW (no horizontal overflow), 0 pageerrors; /story-visuals-preview renders 22 canvases (20 engine types + 2 hero cards) with 0 console errors on desktop, all types now visibly fill their cards (before: tiny center blobs, mostly-empty grid); both story nodes render with live enrichment data — c6 hero shows its own 3-beat walkthrough timeline in the right half, c12 psy "Schizophrenia" likewise, idea-scene generic-flow boxes clean. Remaining console entries on story pages are NOT this pass: (a) atlas-sw/sw.js 404 — the PWA pass's hand-written service worker is absent from the dev tree (their artifact; registration warning only), (b) WebGL "GPU stall due to ReadPixels" — driver perf warnings inherent to STORY-2's frame-extraction architecture, present in baseline too. tsc --noEmit: 0 errors in all 5 touched files (repo-wide 144 errors are pre-existing debt in other agents' files — simulations/app pages; count moves as they edit). ## STUDENT-C10

- [fixed-c10fix] (2026-09-20 17:05-19:10 UTC) C10 PASS COMPLETE — grammar bank leak + pre-board honesty + identity/branding + honest-Hindi. ROOT CAUSE (P1 pair): practice slugs "grammar"/"computer" missed `_CORPUS_SUBJECTS` (corpus codes "gram"/"comp") → corpus lane returned 0 → `_get_generators_for_subject`'s SUBJECT-BLIND first-10-registered fallback served the class10 appmath generator 100% of the time; the same generator family also matched "mathematics" (module contains "math") and a gen-agent seeding run (`backend/tools/insert_generator_variants.py`) had persisted 43 out-of-syllabus propositional-logic rows into board_questions (class 10, subject='math', source_type='generator', node_tags=[]) which the math pre-board served verbatim — the Q21-24 text/options mismatch rows were BANK rows whose embedded option blocks don't match their own options arrays. FIXES: (1) selector: grammar/computer corpus aliases; CHAPTER gating — select_next/_get_candidates/_corpus_candidates take topic_ids, chapter-scoped pools win when they can fill, generators NEVER serve a chapter-scoped session, competency-filter degrades on thin chapter pools, exclude_question_ids stops deterministic-winner repeats (practice.py passes the session's seen set in both the pre-generation loop and _select_adaptive_next); subject-gated generator lane (subject-blind fallback REMOVED; appmath/applied excluded from mathematics). (2) DATA: 43 class-10 logic generator rows DELETEd from board_questions (shared postgres — effective on both backends immediately, verified live pre-restart: fresh math paper already 0 logic / 0 broken); stale "Pre-Board 2025" exam titles retitled. (3) preboard.py: _is_logic_item net (high-precision markers incl. symbol chips + all-operator option sets; bare "contradiction"/"connective" deliberately NOT matched — proof-by-contradiction and connective tissue are real syllabus content), _has_broken_options rejects text/array option mismatches and duplicate chips, true_false rows served as REAL objective chips (options ["True","False"] — the stored key is an exact "True"/"False", nothing invented), honest Section A relabel ("Objective" only when option-bearing items actually exist; "Objective & Very Short Answer" mixed label; "Very Short Answer" + explicit shortfall when none), _take now respects section kind priority (the old source_type sort let alphabetical-'competency' 5-mark long_answers fill Section A), honest map-skills disclosure on every c10 soc-sci paper. (6) enhanced_nodes /stats: new per_class_subject counts. (7) FRONTEND (deploy/frontend, mirrored to frontend/; shipped by the next image build — the concurrent grade-map agent's 18:46 UTC rebuild carried it): exam-simulator branding board-aware from /api/auth/me (hero "Real-time simulator for CBSE Board Exams", ready-screen "{board} · " or "Sample paper ·", board-neutral tip/footer — no more "ISC" to a CBSE account); tutor page binds Class to the signed-in student's real class_level (auth user → /api/auth/me fetch; chip omitted entirely when unknown, payload omits class instead of claiming 11); diagnostic page hides subject cards with ZERO class corpus nodes via /api/nodes/stats per_class_subject (Hindi hidden for class 10, kept for classes 11/12 which have 240/255 hin_a nodes; fail-open before stats load).  — Class 10 synthetic-student audit (2026-09-20, prod :3001, fresh Class 10 signup)

- [STUDENT-C10][P1 MCQ-TEXT-OPTIONS-MISMATCH] (prod :3001) Class 10 Mathematics pre-board paper serves broken/unanswerable MCQs: Section A Q21-24 question TEXT embeds one option set ("A) The Ganga river flows through Varanasi / B) The opposite of the above / C) Both A and B / D) Neither A nor B") while the served `options` array is a DIFFERENT set ("A) It is not a proposition / B) Only in certain cases / C) It is a proposition / D) It cannot be determined"), so the rendered UI shows text-options then five alien chips (observed live in Playwright: "D) Neither A nor B | AA) It is a propo..."); Q24 additionally duplicates "It cannot be determined" as options A and D. 4 consecutive broken 1-mark questions in every generated math paper (ids vary per session; deterministic from bank). Payload: POST /api/preboard/mathematics/start?class_level=10 -> sections[0].questions[20..23]. Fix direction: bank rows whose question_text carries inline options must have those stripped or their options array regenerated from the same row; add a paper-builder invariant text-options == options array.
- [STUDENT-C10][P1 OFFSYLL-LOGIC-BANK] (prod :3001) A propositional-logic (Boolean algebra) bank leaks into Class 10 surfaces where the topic is NOT in the CBSE class 10 syllabus: (a) GRAMMAR practice is 100% logic - all 6 chapters c10_gram_cbse_90..95_001 (tenses/modals/reported speech/passive) serve only "The symbol '→' represents the _____ connective" and "'I will pass AND I will not pass' -> tautology/contradiction" items (13/13 questions across 6 probes, question ids C10-APP-069-...); (b) the math PRE-BOARD paper carries >=12 such questions (~15 marks incl. a 5-mark truth-table question D40 and 4 'Which of the following is a proposition?' MCQs A21-24); (c) the MATHEMATICS diagnostic's Q1 is "The implication p → q is logically equivalent to _____ √ q" (confirmed in the real UI); (d) Political Science, Social Science and Computer Science diagnostics serve the same '∧' connective item. Source: deploy/backend/solvers/appmath/class10_appmath_questions.py (ids C10-APP-*) + 29 logic-flavored class-10 rows in board_questions (28 math, 1 bio, by ILIKE probe). Cross-ref [STUDENT-C1112] off-class leak (C10-APP in c11 physics diagnostic) - same root cause, fixes must land together.
- [STUDENT-C10][P1 PREBOARD-OBJECTIVE-SECTION] (prod :3001) Class 10 Science and Social Science pre-board papers have ZERO option-bearing questions: 49/49 questions ship with `options: []` while Section A is titled "Section A — Objective, All questions compulsory. 1 mark each" (20x1). CBSE reality: Science Section A = 20 objective (MCQ/assertion-reason), Social Science Section A = 20 MCQs incl. map items. Assertion-reason items exist but render as typed subjective answers; soc-sci paper also has no map-skills question (only "map" hit is a Bhakra Nangal reading passage). The paper skeleton promises the board format; the bank cannot deliver it. Fix direction: tag/generate objective rows for class-10 science/soc-sci banks and make the builder fall back honestly (shortfall note) instead of silently filling an "Objective" section with long-answer rows.
- [STUDENT-C10][P2 CT-NO-MCQ] (prod :3001) Chapter tests contain no MCQs at all for Class 10: math ch01 live run = 20 questions, 0 with options (fill_blank 8, true_false 2 with empty options - the child must TYPE "True", short_answer 9, long_answer 1); identical pattern across all 13 live runs in 9 subjects. The objective section of the boards is therefore never trained anywhere outside the (broken) pre-board MCQs. Fix direction: chapter-test builder should prefer option-bearing rows for the 1-mark block, same invariant as pre-board.
- [STUDENT-C10][P2 DIAG-ANSWER-500] (prod :3001) POST /api/diagnostics/{session_id}/answer returns 500 "Internal Server Error" (unhandled crash, not the clean 400 "Question mismatch" path) for stale/mismatched question ids on several class-10 subjects: Science, Chemistry, Political Science, Social Science, English, Computer Science each crashed on an answer during full 8-question sweeps (p4_diag_tutor.jsonl diag_result fails: '500:"Internal Server Error"'), which then blocks submit/result ("Diagnostic not yet complete"). The real UI splices server-issued next questions and avoids the path, but any stale client (offline replay, retry after 30-min token refresh) hits a raw 500. Fix direction: wrap the engine's mismatch ValueError into the same 400 the router already has.
- [STUDENT-C10][P2 SUBJECT-CARD-EMPTY] (prod :3001) Surfaces advertise subjects Class 10 cannot serve: diagnostic UI shows a Hindi card -> 503 "Question bank returned no questions for this subject"; /api/chapter-tests/chapters -> 400 "Unknown subject 'computer'" and "Unknown subject 'grammar'" although both have teaching nodes (16 gram / 8 comp) - 2 of 11 subjects cannot be chapter-tested and one cannot run a diagnostic. Fix direction: hide cards without banks (or return empty-catalog 200s) so the child never starts into an error.
- [STUDENT-C10][P3 EXAM-SIM-ISC] (prod :3001) /exam-simulator hero says "Real-time simulator for ISC Board Exams" and lists "Science Pre-Board 2025" papers to a CBSE class-10 account (shot 07_exam_simulator.png) - wrong board and stale year on a board-prep surface; /exam-prep (the CBSE pre-board simulator) is correctly CBSE-branded.

- [STUDENT-C10][P3 MASTERY-MAP-SIZE] (prod :3001) /api/progress/mastery for a fresh Class 10 account totals 6 nodes (Science 3, Mathematics 2, English 1) against a 660-node syllabus (/api/nodes/by-class-subject sums 660; galaxy screen says "0/660 stars") - the dashboard tells the child the syllabus is ~1% of its real size. Fix direction: mastery map should aggregate the full class catalog like galaxy does.
- [STUDENT-C10][P3 TRANSIENT-500s] (prod :3001) Intermittent 500s under concurrent load, each clean on immediate retry (child-visible flake): POST /api/practice/start for c10_gram_cbse_95_001 (500 -> 201), GET /api/content/nodes/c10_bio_cbse_01_011/enrichment (500 -> 200), GET /api/content/nodes/c10_math_cbse_01_001/questions (500 -> 200), one POST /api/preboard/{sid}/submit (422 -> 200 with identical payload), one /progress page resource 500 (not reproducible). - [STUDENT-C9][P1 DIAG-LAST-ANSWER-500] (prod :3001) Class 9 diagnostics can never complete server-side: the last served question's answer always 500s ("AttributeError: plan exhausted", deploy/backend/api/diagnostics.py:265 get_next_question via diagnostic_service._irt_select) in all 9 subjects (answered stops at 4/5, submit refuses "Diagnostic incomplete: 4/5", result 400) AND with the exact UI sequence (4x POST /answer then batch POST /submit with all 5 records, as app/(student)/diagnostic/page.tsx does) the submit itself 500s Internal Server Error. No Class 9 diagnostic session can ever reach result/misconceptions/remediation. Fix direction: treat plan-tail exhaustion as completion (return stored verdict + diagnostic_complete) instead of raising; wrap the engine's exhaust path.
- [STUDENT-C9][P1 CT-WRONG-CLASS-RACE] (prod :3001) /chapter-test renders the CLASS 10 catalog to a logged-in Class 9 child during load: on hard navigation the auth store has not rehydrated when the page mounts, `myClass = Number(user?.class_level) || 10` (chapter-test/page.tsx:133) initializes to 10 and the first GET /api/chapter-tests/chapters fires with class_level=10; the deep-link ?class=9 fetch lands seconds later (network-logged both calls; screenshot shows Class 10 chapters "The Fundamental Theorem of Arithmetic / A Quadratic Equation / The Line Of Sight" rendered under ?class=9). A child clicking during the window starts a Class 10 test from the Class 9 screen. Fix direction: block render on auth hydration and read user.class_level reactively (same init pattern already flagged by C6).
- [STUDENT-C9][P2 CT-PHYSICS-CODE-SPLIT] (prod :3001) Class 9 physics chapter tests are unreachable by a subject-code split: board_questions stores class-9 physics under subject='physics' (1,272 questions, chapters ch1..ch6+ch01..ch06, verified via Postgres) while _SUBJECT_CODES maps 'physics'->['phy'] (deploy/backend/api/chapter_tests.py:53) -> catalog 200 {"chapters":[]}, start 503 "0 available", UI "No question bank chapters available for this subject and class yet"; the combined 'science' catalog (['phy','chem','bio']) also drops ALL class-9 physics chapters, so Science tests cover only bio+chem. Fix direction: accept both codes in _SUBJECT_CODES or normalize board_questions.subject for c9_physics rows.
- [STUDENT-C9][P2 CT-DUPLICATE-CHAPTERS] (prod :3001) Every Class 9 chapter-test catalog lists each chapter twice: two ingestion pipelines file the same chapters as ch1..ch8 (node-derived, titled, 147-172q) and ch01..ch08 (practice-derived, untitled, 25-51q) -> math shows 16 cards for 8 real chapters ("Chapter 1 · The Cartesian Coordinate System 159q" + "Chapter 1 41q"), all 11 subjects affected. Fix direction: normalize chapter codes (zero-pad or merge) and backfill titles for the practice-pipeline rows.
- [STUDENT-C9][P2 CT-SCIENCE-SUBJECT-MERGE] (prod :3001) Class 9 "Science" chapter tests merge different subjects by chapter number: "Chapter 1 · The Discovery Of The Cell" = 352q (bio cell 182 + chem mixtures 170), "Chapter 2 · Subatomic Particles" = 325q (chem 155 + bio tissues 170) - a child gets a cross-subject quiz labelled as one chapter. Fix direction: build the science catalog per (subject, chapter) pair, not chapter code alone.
- [STUDENT-C9][P2 PRACTICE-POOL-DEGENERATE] (prod :3001) Class 9 practice pools collapse to a few questions: 3x10-question sessions yield 5 unique questions (bio), 4 (engl), 7 (math), 16 (english), 0 (hindi 503); sessions also serve fewer than requested (bio 1, engl 2 of 6). Adaptive practice is impossible (stage345_fix.json). Same class as [STUDENT-C8][P2 PRACTICE-POOL].
- [STUDENT-C9][P2 TUTOR-EMPTY-ANSWERS] (prod :3001) /api/tutor-graph/ask returns 200 with an EMPTY answer for Class 9 chemistry (43.8s), economics (34.6s) and Hindi (1.4s) (stage3457_functional.json); doubts on NCERT-9 topics the corpus lacks get silently answered from unrelated nodes (French Revolution doubt -> "social change classified by pace"; Himalayan vs peninsular rivers -> generic physiography). Also the tutor ITS WebSocket ws://.../api/tutor/its/ws fails the handshake (ERR_INVALID_HTTP_RESPONSE) on every /tutor load.
- [STUDENT-C9][P2 STORY-COLD-503] (prod :3001) Cold enrichment fetches 503 in bursts and StoryMode turns that into a dead-end: 49/416 class-9 nodes returned 503 on a 6-way parallel first fetch (0 failures after warm), and 2 of 12 first-view browser story loads showed "Story unavailable. We couldn't load the lesson..." (c9_math_ch1_11, c9_physics_ch4_11), each fine on retry - a classroom opening a new chapter simultaneously hits the dead-end card. - [STUDENT-C9][P3 DIAG-BANK-OFFGRADE] (prod :3001) The diagnostic UI's local fallback bank is class-agnostic and off-grade for Class 9: chapter chips Algebra/Geometry/Trigonometry/Calculus (app/(student)/diagnostic/page.tsx chapters_math) with correctAnswer in the client bundle; the served English plan also mixes a Class 7 bank (400 "Question mismatch. - [STUDENT-C9][P3 LEARN-DEFAULT-CLASS] (prod :3001) /learn initializes classLevel to 10 and never reads user.class_level (learn/page.tsx:48), so a fresh Class 9 child lands on Class 10 topics; the enrichment list response also never sets has_eli5 (always false although eli5 is populated), so the ELI5 badge cannot render; Hindi/CS tabs render "0 topics available" instead of being hidden.

- [STUDENT-C15][P1 PLAY-CATALOG-NOT-SHIPPED] (prod :3001) THE ENTIRE JUNIOR GAMES CATALOG IS UNREACHABLE IN PRODUCTION. /play fetches /data/games/_manifest.json (frontend/lib/game-loader.ts:23,87); that path returns the Next.js 404 page because deploy/frontend/public/data/ does not exist — neither in the deploy source tree nor in the running dream-frontend image (/app/public contains only doodle_cards, icons, manifest.json, sims, sw.js). loadManifest() swallows the 404 and returns [], so the page renders "Pick Your Adventure!" with class chips but ZERO subject cards and ZERO games — all 133 junior games (which exist only in the dev copy frontend/public/data/games/) are invisible to a real student. The shipped image also contains public/sims that deploy/frontend/public lacks, so deploy tree and running image have diverged in both directions (sync rule in AGENTS.md not followed for this feature). Fix direction: copy frontend/public/data/games/ into deploy/frontend/public/data/games/ and rebuild the frontend image (one build per batch); verify with curl http://localhost:3001/data/games/_manifest.json -> 200 and /play subject cards rendering.
- [STUDENT-C15][P2 ENGLISH-SUBJECT-EMPTY] SUBJ-advertised English has 0 of 133 games in every class 1-5. SUBJECT_META (play/page.tsx:43) advertises "📖 English", but no junior game teaches letters, phonics, reading, or grammar; the chip can never render because subjectsAvailable is derived from games' filename-derived subject keys only. - [STUDENT-C15][P2 PLACE-VALUE-TEMPLATE] Class 1-2 math slides render a CRORES/LAKHS place-value chart with static digits "1234" on every place-value slide, contradicting the slide content: c1_CountingGame ("5 apples", teen numbers), c1_AdditionGame, c1_PatternsGame, c2_NumbersGame (numbers to 999 — diagram shows a 4-digit number in crore columns). A 6-year-old learning to count to 20 is shown crore/lakh place value; the diagram data (JSON diagram.data columns/digits) is a shared template never customized per game/slide. Fix direction: generate the chart from each slide's actual numbers (or drop the diagram for c1/c2).
- [STUDENT-C15][P3 GAMES-METADATA-DRIFT] (a) _manifest.json `subject` is undefined for all 133 entries (the page masks this by re-deriving subject from filenames via getSubjectKey regexes — content labels and UI labels never match: game JSONs say physics/biology/chemistry/geography, UI says science/evs). (b) 29 of 133 games have EMPTY cisceNodes (12 of 28 C4 games incl. c4_FractionsGame; 17 of 33 C5 games incl. c5_IntegersGame, c5_LatLongGame) — more than half the C5 catalog has no syllabus-node linkage. (c) CountingGame's "Which is More?" slide says 14 vs 9 apples but its bar-chart diagram plots 40 vs 60.

## CHILD-LENS — child experience audit (2026-09-21, prod :3001)

Fresh-signup journeys walked as real students: Class 6, Class 9, Class 12 (3 separate accounts, headless Chrome over :3001). Screenshots + visible-text + link inventories at `D:\platform\_shots\childlens\` (c6, c6_mobile, c6_explore, c6_lesson, c6_quiz, c6_practice3, c6_diagfull, c6_explore2, c6_after2, c9, c9_explore, c12, c12_explore). Full narrative: `D:\platform\CHILD_EXPERIENCE_GAPS.md`. These are the product defects from that walk (UX copy + wiring; no code changed by this agent).

- [CHILD-LENS][P1 EXPLORE-EMPTY-THEN-C10] (prod :3001) The dashboard mission's own CTA dead-ends: "Explore the map" -> /explore shows "Knowledge Graph — 0 nodes · 0 links — Building knowledge graph…" for ~10s (300-node graph fetch), then renders a hard-coded Class 10 graph for EVERY account: `explore/page.tsx` `const [classLevel, setClassLevel] = useState<number>(10)` — verified a C6, a C9 and a C12 fresh account all saw the same C10 nodes (Photosynthesis, IUPAC nomenclature, Contraception, Horace Danby). Class picker is unlabeled bare digits 1-12. Day-1 child journey = broken promise + wrong-age content on the app's headline action.
- [CHILD-LENS][P1 LEARN-C6-SEES-C12] (prod :3001) /learn hub shows Class 12 physics (Ohm's Law, Lens formula, Nuclear Fission, Electromagnetic Induction) to a signed-in Class 6 child; same `useState(10)` root as [STUDENT-C9][P3 LEARN-DEFAULT-CLASS] but the C6 evidence is worse: every card also renders Bloom-taxonomy tags "apply/understand/analyze" + "medium", and the header link is `chapter-test?subject=physics&class=10`. To an 11-year-old the page reads as "not for me" within seconds.
- [CHILD-LENS][P1 DIAGNOSTIC-DEAD-START] (prod :3001) /diagnostic: "Start Diagnostic" is `disabled` (`canStart = subject !== "" && chapter !== ""`, diagnostic/page.tsx:818) and NOTHING tells the child a chapter must also be picked — tapping a subject then Start looks broken (grey button, `cursor-not-allowed`, no tooltip). Worse, the chapter list for Mathematics is class-blind (Algebra/Geometry/Trigonometry/Calculus for every class incl. Class 6). Entry copy promises "about 15 minutes" but the run is 5 questions; per-question countdown timer (~3:00) with alarm icon reads as danger, and the tiny ✕ is the only exit.
- [CHILD-LENS][P1 LESSON-CTA-GOES-TO-TUTOR] (prod :3001) On a lesson page the PRIMARY gold button "Start Learning" is `<Link href="/tutor">` (learn/[topicId]/page.tsx) — a child clicking it to start the lesson is dumped into the Socratic chat instead of the lesson content that is already on the page. Secondary "Take Quiz" toggles an inline Quick Practice that sat on "Picking a verified question…" (spinner) >3.5s during the walk.
- [CHILD-LENS][P2 TUTOR-FAKE-MASTERY-AND-C12-CHIP] (prod :3001) /tutor greets a fresh Class 6 child with "Mathematics · Class 6 · Mastery: 30%" — a hard-coded default (`useState(masteryProp ?? 0.3)`, tutor/page.tsx) while dashboard/progress honestly show 0% ("Nothing estimated"). Suggested chips are static strings incl. "Derivatives samajh nahi aaye" (s_derivatives) — a Class 12 word offered to a C6 child regardless of subject; entire tutor UI is Hinglish even when the child picked EN (the EN/हिं toggle doesn't switch it).
- [CHILD-LENS][P2 DASHBOARD-DEFICIT-MISSION] (prod :3001) After a child's FIRST practice session (5 questions, 2 XP), the dashboard mission flips to "chemistry is trailing at 0%. Lowest of your subjects right now — one focused session moves the needle." (CommandBriefing trailingTitle/trailingSub) and the review row says "1 topic is due for review — clear them before they slip", while MASTERY still reads 0% "No reading yet — a diagnostic sets the baseline". Effort -> deficit headline + unmoved meter = "this isn't helping me" churn moment (screenshot _shots/childlens/c6_after2/01_dashboard_after_real_session.png).
- [CHILD-LENS][P2 ONBOARDING-ORPHANED] (prod :3001) The 5-step /onboarding welcome is unreachable: signup hard-pushes /dashboard (`signup/page.tsx` router.push('/dashboard')) and grep finds zero references to "/onboarding" in app/ or components/. A fresh child therefore gets no tour; the dashboard offers three competing first actions (mission CTA -> broken /explore; 18 Start cards; "Take your first diagnostic" buried at the page bottom).
- [CHILD-LENS][P2 ACHIEVEMENTS-DUPLICATE-BADGES] (prod :3001) Badge wall shows duplicated/conflicting badges: "First Steps — Complete your first lesson" twice; "Week Warrior" twice with DIFFERENT definitions ("14-day review streak" vs "7-day streak"); "Topic Tamer" and "Concept Builder" identical ("Master 10 knowledge nodes", 0/10 both); "Month Master" ≈ "Monthly Dedication" (30-day). Also "Speed Demon — Answer 10 questions in under 5 seconds each" rewards racing over thinking, and copy uses "spaced-repetition reviews"/"knowledge nodes"/"board-level exam". Children read a duplicated wall as fake.
- [CHILD-LENS][P2 EXAM-PREP-SILENT-CLASS-MAP] (prod :3001) /exam-prep maps every sub-10 class to Class 10 papers (`myClass < 10 ? 10 : myClass`) with no child-facing note, so a Class 9 account lands on "CLASS Class 10" 3h/80-mark board papers presented as their exam prep (false for C9); a C12 account defaults to the C10 toggle too. Intro copy is examiner-speak ("internal choice where the question bank supports it").
- [CHILD-LENS][P3 LESSON-CHROME-ADULTS] (prod :3001) Lesson page chrome contradicts its good content: tabs "Overview / Diagnostics / Remediation / Scope"; header shows raw "medium / understand" tags; "Simple Explanation (ELI5)" is VERBATIM identical to the definition above it (derive_eli5 output not simplified); "No doodle card exported for this node yet." (pipeline message) rendered to the child; Prerequisites and "This Unlocks" rows truncated mid-sentence with no expand ("Comparing numbers is the process of d…").
- [CHILD-LENS][P3 JUNIOR-MODE-ORPHANED] (prod :3001) /junior (pastel mascot, 4 big subject cards, "Aaj hum kya seekhenge?") is the only age-appropriate surface for young children and NOTHING links to it (grep: zero inbound refs); its "Hing" language chip wears a padlock with no explanation. C6-7 children never see it.
- [CHILD-LENS][P3 SIGNUP-CLASS-DEFAULT-10] (prod :3001) Signup class dropdown defaults to Class 10 (signup/page.tsx `useState(10)`); one unnoticed tap mis-ages the entire product for a younger child. /my-galaxy also opens on ALL classes ("0 of 9,099 stars lit") instead of the child's own class tab, and the legend ("gold = mastered · faint = not yet — an honest sky") is opaque to an 11-year-old.
- [CHILD-LENS][P3 CHAPTER-TEST-MACHINE-TITLES] (prod :3001) /chapter-test lists machine-generated node titles as chapter names for C12 Mathematics: "A Relation R On A Set A", "A Matrix", "A Determinant", "A Real-Valued Function (F)" plus "327 questions in the bank" — database rows where children expect chapter names ("Relations and Functions", "Matrices", "Determinants").
- [CHILD-LENS][P3 NO-GLOBAL-NAV] (prod :3001) There is no persistent navigation on any student surface: /practice's link inventory contains exactly ONE anchor ("Skip to content"); /progress, /practice, lesson pages and /diagnostic are mutually unreachable except via browser Back. A child who finishes a practice session has no visible route to their progress, another subject, or home (dashboard links.json: 26 links, none to /practice /diagnostic /progress).

## PEDAGOGY-AUDIT — teaching-layer audit (2026-09-21, prod :3001, fresh Class 6 signup + code walk of all teaching surfaces)

Full report: `D:\platform\TEACHING_LAYER_GAPS.md`. Evidence: `D:\platform\_pedaudit\shots\c6\` (screenshots + DOM text dumps + full diagnostic transcript). Overlaps with the same-day CHILD-LENS walk are cross-referenced, not re-filed. Auditor only — no code changed.

- [PEDAGOGY-AUDIT][P0 API-OUTAGE] (prod :3001) BOTH backends hung for 50+ minutes mid-audit: dream-backend-1/2 recreated ~16:08 UTC, app never binds — last log line is the MiniLM load ("BertModel LOAD REPORT" after "unauthenticated requests to the HF Hub"), then silence; `GET /api/health` 502 (nginx: connect() refused to BOTH upstreams), `docker exec` into backend-1 times out, RestartCount=0, status flapping healthy->unhealthy. Startup appears deadlocked after the embedding-model load (no port bind, no further logs). Impact: entire API down (auth, practice, review, diagnostic all dead) for every student. Ops: needs a restart + a startup watchdog/timeout around model load (backend/ai/model_hub.py load path + whichever initializer calls it); poller transcript in `_pedaudit` session log 21:59–22:27 IST window.
- [PEDAGOGY-AUDIT][P1 LEARN-BROWSE-502] (prod :3001) `GET /api/content/enrichment` returns nginx 502 on every params combo tried (subject=physics/mathematics × class_level=6/10 × limit=10/20/500) on two separate occasions ~40 min apart, and `GET /api/content/search/enrichment` 502'd on repeat after first succeeding. Consequence: the whole /learn Knowledge Graph browse page is dead in production — a fresh student sees "API error 500: Internal Server Error" + "0 topics available" + "No topics found" (screenshot `shots/c6/50_learn_list.png`). Same endpoints work in backend logs earlier the same day (200s at ~15:5x), so this is a reproducible production failure, not a cold start.
- [PEDAGOGY-AUDIT][P1 DIAG-CLASS-BLIND-BANK] (prod :3001) The diagnostic's item content is class-blind: a fresh **Class 6** account selecting Mathematics→Algebra was served slope/y-intercept, polynomial expansion `(x+3)(x−2)` and `x²−5x+6=0` (Class 9/10 CBSE) — root cause `app/(student)/diagnostic/page.tsx`: hardcoded chapter array `["Algebra","Geometry","Trigonometry","Calculus"]` (line 30) + static fallback bank (lines 262+) with no class_level filter, shown to every class 1–12. Result: "20% mastery / Needs Practice: Quadratic Equations" for an 11-year-old (screenshots `07b_diag_chapters.png`, `10_diag_q*.png`, `31_diag_results.png`). Prior walk flagged the class-blind chapter chips ([CHILD-LENS][P1 DIAGNOSTIC-DEAD-START]); this entry is the deeper bank+scoring half. Fix: serve diagnostic items from `board_questions`/enrichment filtered by the signup `class_level` (columns exist + indexed, models/enrichment.py:106–121); keep static bank as labeled offline fallback only.
- [PEDAGOGY-AUDIT][P2 DIAG-GRADES-CORRECT-WRONG] (prod :3001) Numeric diagnostic answer `x²−5x+6=0` answered `2` (a correct root) was graded WRONG ("0 of 1 correct") with no explanation shown — strict string equality rejects equivalent forms (`2` vs `x=2` vs `2 or 3`). In the same run, MCQ option strings render raw LaTeX (`$y = 2x + 3$`) while stems render KaTeX, and any authored answer containing `$…$` will also fail QuickQuiz's `.trim()` equality compare (components/learn/QuickQuiz.tsx pickOption). Fix: normalize free-text (strip "x=", "$…$", case/space-fold; accept any root for multi-root items) + render options through the KaTeX path the practice page already imports.

### Follow-on solver fixes (same pass)

- [fixed] 'density = 2' LHS labels bound positionally to mass — labeled binder now accepts formula LHS vars as targets; verified (a) 6; (b) 1.76.
- [fixed] 'm3'/'s2' unit-suffix digits leaked as positional numbers — unit-glued digits now excluded spans.
- [fixed] KE-inverse misbind: '4500 joules' rebound to mass by unit-family guess — slots-only rearrange now runs before candidate merges; mass = 10 kg verified.
- [known] Prose calculus ('differentiate x squared plus 3x') can misparse to arithmetic (3²=9) — standard notation (x^2 + 3x) routes correctly to the calculus engine. Canonicalizer doesn't cover calculus ops yet.
- [known] Embedding route noise: 'accelerates at 2 for 5' can land on Exponents (computes 2^5 honestly for that concept); alt-route retry only fires when the primary concept can't execute.

## ROADMAP — non-STEM verification + photo input (2026-09-22)

### Architecture decisions (agreed)
- LLM = layer 3 only (extraction/formatting). Answers come from layer 1 (corpus) or layer 2 (deterministic compute). Verification gate = re-derivation, not model opinion.
- Laya (convaiinnovations, Apache-2.0, 421M, self-hosted): shortlist re-ranker after MiniLM (embeddings for recall over 1,027 concepts, Laya for precision on <=15 options). Jev rejected: closed API, paid, unnecessary at our label size.
- 27B-class model (gemma-4-26b-a4b-it): FALLBACK ONLY — extraction quality + grounded conceptual answers. Never the VERIFIED path.
- Photo input: tesseract first (free, in image) -> flash-lite vision fallback. Feeds same pipeline. Lands on concept node, not answer dump.

### Trust tiers (label honestly in UI)
- VERIFIED = deterministic compute chain
- VERIFIED (canonicalized) = LLM-extracted slots, deterministically computed
- MODEL ANSWER = authored corpus/board key (non-STEM)
- RUBRIC FEEDBACK = AI-scored vs authored marking scheme (never VERIFIED)
- GUIDED = authored method/steps, no final answer
- Refusal = honest abstain + story link

### TODO
- [ ] NONSTEM-1: audit authored-answer coverage per subject (diag answers exist per node; check board_questions.answer coverage)
- [ ] NONSTEM-2: build MODEL ANSWER lane — retrieve authored answer from node/board key for fact-recall

- [ ] NONSTEM-4: bench non-STEM the way maths was benched — N history/geo/english questions -> does authored answer route + grade correctly

[fixed-cisce-solver-commerce] bugs found and closed in this wave (all caught before ship except (3), which shipped earlier and is synced now):
1. [fixed] APC_APS_IDENTITIES answered delta-MPC asks with a level APC ("income rises 1000→1200, consumption 800→950, calculate MPC and APC" shipped "APC = 0.8, APS = 0.2" — a different question). Gate added in _c_apc_aps: a rises-from/rises-from pair + MPC keyword refuses so the books2 MPC_DELTA row takes it.
2. [fixed] New ISC econ families (_c_mpc_delta, _c_mu_schedule) returned 5-tuples — the dispatcher's 6-slot unpack dropped their quantities dict into the wrong slot, so the independent verifier saw empty quantities. Both now return 6-tuples (value, steps, ans, None, equation, quantities).

4. [fixed] MU schedule parser misbound value-before-ordinal schedules ("20 utils from the 1st ice cream" parsed TU_1=45). Backward form ("N utils from the Mth") now parsed first; forward form kept; ordinal suffix "2th"→proper st/nd/rd/th via module-level _ordinal (no dict literals inside f-strings — runtime predates PEP 701).
5. [fixed-dev] New-code bugs caught by the suite before any ship: `taxes?` regex matches "taxe"+s not "tax"+es (→ `tax(?:es)?`); capex clause regex stopped at the colon it needed to cross; CFO working-capital items missed the "trade receivables/payables" qualifier; re.match without re.I made capital-R "Rs" fail in the opening-capital rollup and silently shift every balance by one account (the honesty gate refused, so nothing shipped wrong).

1. [fixed] B06 unit mismatch — cardiac output answered "5250 mL/min" for a "in litres per minute" ask. unit_shape.normalize_to_ask_unit converts to the ASK-named unit (exact factors, inversion-checked); ambiguous asks refuse. After: 5.25 CORRECT.

Tightened: data-coverage guard now applies to the B>A fallback result, and a verified series-resistor chain (sum R, I=V/R_eq) rescues all-values-consumed reads. After: 1 A CORRECT.

[claimed-eli5-pilot 2026-09-30] — ELI5-PILOT agent: rewrite the broken ELI5 header corpus-wide, pilot band first. Audit finding (quality_audit_20260930.py): the corpus-wide `eli5` metric is presence-only (word count >=25), so it reads 100 while the actual ELI5 bodies are the DEFINITION copy-pasted with "In simple words:" prefixed and often the COMMON_MISCONCEPTION stitched in — CISCE nodes (real analogies: "elimination works like cancelling a tennis match", timetable/pressure-cooker/thali) show the intended bar. Pilot scope: worst c9/c10 science band = CHEMISTRY (audit combos all tie at eli5=100; tiebreak = lowest overall score + largest n -> CBSE|c10|chem 66.6/56 + CBSE|c9|chem 31) = 87 files (mission cap 120). All 87 backed up to _cisce_build/eli5_pilot/backup/<name>.orig (_MANIFEST.json + md5 spot-check); 5 authoring batches, RESUME_STATE.json checkpointed after each (87/87 done). Each ELI5 hand-authored from the node's own DEFINITION+WALKTHROUGH (+THIS_FORMULA) — everyday-analogy opener, plain language, 40-120 words, no prefix, no misconception stitching, zero node ids. Replacement is parse-safe: only the body under `## ELI5` touched; byte-diff proof that everything from `## DEFINITION` onward is identical for all 87 (automated prefix+tail identity check, 0 failures). Per-file verification (0 hard-gate failures): word count 62-89 (mean 76.6); zero "in simple words" anywhere in any file; zero node-id pattern hits; zero numbers in new ELI5s absent from the node's DEFINITION/WALKTHROUGH/THIS_FORMULA (grounding check caught 1 real defect during authoring — c10_chem_cbse_05_006 carried digits from a sibling node, rephrased; also 1 wording tightening on c10_chem_cbse_02_001); novel-token tripwire (words not in node sources) reviewed per batch — all remaining flags are analogy vocabulary or morphology artifacts (hyphens, Na vs sodium, galvanisation vs galvanization), zero invented chemistry facts. Honest self-grade on 10 random files vs the CISCE exemplar bar (full node re-read): distribution 8/8/8/9/9/9/9/9/9/10 (mean 8.8) — 8s are solid-but-not-memorable (list-y middles, walkthrough-observation openers rather than invented similes); the 10 (c9_chem_ch3_5 ionic bonding) matches CISCE voice (kerosene-stored sodium meets toxic yellow-green chlorine, out comes table salt). Defects found in the corpus during the pilot: (a) audit `eli5` metric measures word count only and therefore masks the definition-restated pattern corpus-wide — recommend the wave use the pilot's verifier (prefix check + grounding + tail-identity) as the gate; (b) c10_chem_cbse_01_002/04_004-style nodes embed the misconception INSIDE the old ELI5 ("Common mix-up: ...") — stitching pattern is real and now gone in the pilot band. 4. Serving: routers/compq.py (deploy+root) — GET /api/compq/node/{id} (CBQs question-side only: no guideline/gates/traps in listings; embedded CQs; worked examples full; diagnostics q-only), POST /api/compq/attempt (numeric: deterministic gate-matching vs authored Step-4 numbers, unit-aware 2% tol, conservative verdicts correct>=0.75/partial>=0.33; solver cross-check ONLY via services/solve_isolation.run_async with honest budget/saturation degradation; theory: authored Guideline + rubric self-assessment; miss surfaces named Traps + nearest diagnostic's misconception carve; honest nulls), GET /api/compq/variants/{id} (authored V1-V5 as fresh practice instances, mutation text + base question). Node ids scrubbed from every display string at parse time (owner rule) — ids travel only as opaque fetch keys. Loader is memory-lean: 45 MB bank in RAM, the two big files offset-indexed with LRU (no ~500 MB/worker residency), 3.5 s cold load, sub-ms per-node lookups.
5. Mounts: main.py + routers/__init__.py one-line additions both trees (the __init__ export is REQUIRED — without it `from backend.routers import compq_router` binds the module, not the router, and include_router would crash at boot).
6. (3) BIOLOGY (biology_families.py): CARDIAC_OUTPUT abbreviated HR=/SV= slots (septic-shock 6050 mL/min); NEW ALVEOLAR_VENTILATION (TV−dead-space)×rate with 4-variable solve (4200 mL/min verified; plain minute-ventilation still 90000 via BREATHING_VOLUME); NEW BLOOD_VOLUME_INDICES (RBC volume 2.25 L; TBV=plasma/(1−Hct) 5.4545 L; post-plasma-loss Hct 75%); NEW SPORE_COUNTS (sporangia×per-spore 1e7; germination % 40000; per-spore probability; binary-fission 2^n incl. word numbers ⇒ 32); NEW DIHYBRID_COUNTS (9:3:3:1 counts: rryy 40/640, round-yellow 360/640; ask-clause trait extraction ignores parent phenotypes); NEW RECOMBINATION_FREQUENCY (180 parental + 20 recombinant ⇒ 10%; RF 12% of 500 ⇒ 60); CHARGAFF rewritten: nearest-base binding (word-then-number beats stray number-before-word), percent/percentage words, cytosine/thymine/DNA triggers — "%G=24 ⇒ adenine=26%" now correct in value AND label (was "guanine = 26%", judged WRONG). (4) PHYSICS (physics_families.py): CHARGE_CARRIER trigger extended ("charge that passes"); NEW EMF_INTERNAL_RESISTANCE over 31 mined phrasings — I=E/(R+r), V=E−Ir, reverse E=V+Ir, registered BEFORE the ohm row so the 2-of-3 slot read can never drop the external resistor (was shipping "24 A" for a 12 V/0.5 Ω/5.5 Ω cell; correct 2 A, 11 V). [claimed-multipart-splitting 2026-10-01] -> [fixed-multipart-splitting] SHIPPED (code + tests + 52-question honest probe; coordinator ships containers). Standing-gate dominant refusal class: multi-part / narrative questions refused WHOLE even when several parts are computable. Built PART-WISE VERDICTS: NEW deploy/backend/its/solver/part_splitter.py (mirrored to backend/) segments on 5 exam shapes — (a)/(b) paren letters (skips tolerated, best-increasing-run anchored at 'a', immune to set-notation collisions like n(C)), (i)/(ii) roman, line-start Q1./1./2. numbered lists (short lead line = shared scenario), sub-asks (>=2 ask clauses / >=2 ask sentences / compound-object 'Find the equilibrium price and the equilibrium quantity'), and semicolon-separated asks (data lists refuse). Narrative case-study wrappers inherit verbatim as each part's scenario (quantities live in the wrapper). Never-split guards: single questions, MCQ option lists (value-like fragments), assertion-reason / match-the-following / 'choose the correct', 'between X and Y' coordinate pairs, >6 fragments, >4000 chars. Wiring additive-only in api/verified_practice.py /solve: fires ONLY when the whole solve did not verify AND >=2 parts detected (timeout/saturation branch deliberately untouched — availability failure is not a refusal); each part (scenario + sub-ask) solves through the SAME verified path (answer_pipeline.answer under solve_isolation.run_async, own budget + cache); response carries additive payload["multipart"] = {parts:[{part_id, question, tier, answer (VERIFIED parts only), steps, socratic, refusal_reason (guided parts) + guided_teaching when a node binding rode in}], overall: verified|partial|guided} — overall 'verified' only when EVERY part verified on its own merits; a part answer is never promoted by other parts passing; single-part questions gain no key. Honesty proof on 52 REAL multi-part questions (8 standing-gate refused markers B14/C07/C15/A12/C14/CORPUS1-3 + 44 mined from enhanced_nodes_md, 2126 total candidates split cleanly): 51 refused whole before; after, 5 convert to >=1 verified part (CTX17 1/4, CTX20 1/4, N04 2/3, N16 2/3, N18 1/3 — ~10% overall, 3/19 on the numeric-subject batch), 0 fabricated answers (every part answer is a pipeline VERIFIED* label; uncomputable parts stay guided with refusal_reason + authored socratic method content, the same guided quality as the whole-question path). Honest ceiling: conversion is bounded by the pipeline's per-part family coverage — most competency multi-part parts are beyond coverage and stay guided (now with per-part method content instead of one whole-question wall). Splitter-unit suite backend/tests/test_part_splitter.py 29/29 (shapes, scenario inheritance, composite wiring with faked run_async, per-part honesty, no answer on guided parts, timeout honesty, no-multipart-key on singles); smoke 25/25; regressions green: accountancy 73/73, physics 97/97 (of 115), gate_failures 37/37, solve_isolation+solver_contract 32, idor+observability+readable 70. Backups + probes: _cisce_build/multipart_backup/ (RESUME_STATE.json, out/full_results.json, out/batch2_results.json).
[claimed-freefall-time-confident-wrong 2026-10-01] DISCOVERED during the multipart probe (pre-existing, NOT caused by splitting): the physics fallback VERIFIES WRONG on free-fall TIME asks. Repro (deploy tree, solo questions, no multipart): answer("A stone is dropped from a bridge 45 m above a river. (Take g = 10 m/s^2 downward). How long does it take to hit the water?") -> VERIFIED 29.6985 (that is the impact velocity sqrt(2*9.8*45), and with g=9.8 not the stated 10) instead of t = sqrt(2h/g) = 3 s; and answer("A stone is dropped from a 45 m high bridge. Find the time it takes to hit the water. (Take g = 10 m/s^2.)") -> VERIFIED 4.5 s (= h/g, wrong; correct 3 s). Surfaced because part-wise splitting now SHIPS per-part verified answers, so a whole-refused free-fall multi-part item can carry a confident-wrong part. Fix belongs in physics_families.py / the kinematics families (protected files) — coordinator decision needed; the multipart wiring itself relays pipeline verdicts faithfully and fabricates nothing.

DEFECTS FIXED: (1) cold-lane flake — fanning 4+ solves out concurrently raced the one-time singleton init (MiniLM/spacy/formula graph) and the last one past the wall busted its 60s budget into an avoidable honest-GUIDED; fix = warm-start (first question solves solo, remaining gather concurrently; same wall-clock warm, robust on first photo after restart). RESPONSE SHAPE (after): {questions:[{text, bbox:[x0,y0,x1,y1] normalized, tier, solved, answer (verified-only), numeric (verified-only), steps, refusal_reason?, guided_teaching?, repertoire{node_id,doodle,diagram,sim,sim_title}, elapsed_ms, cached}], count, multi_question, overall verified|partial|guided, n_verified, truncated, truncation_note, box_source vision|line_heuristic|even_share, extracted_text, elapsed_ms} — vs /solve-image's flat first-question-mirroring without positions. ## [TEACH-LOOP + CISCE-BANK + LEAK-FIX] 2026-10-08 — post-audit launch batch

### Teaching loop (answer -> understand -> check -> stretch -> next)
- `services/enhanced_nodes.py` :: `teach_loop_for(node_id)` — builds the 4-step loop from
  authored corpus: concept (title/def/eli5) + diagnostic check + `_comp` capstone
  (exam targets + seed title) + `LEADS_TO` forward links with reasons.
- `api/verified_practice.py` :: `_with_repertoire` attaches `teach_loop` on EVERY solve
  outcome (verified/guided/refused) — an unverified answer is the highest-intent
  teaching moment and previously dead-ended at a static refusal.
- Fallback node binding: when no node_id is stamped in the audit trail, a strict
  semantic match (MiniLM cosine >= 0.45 on the node embedding index) binds free-text
  doubts to their concept. Fail-closed: no hit -> `teach_loop: null`.
- `api/competency_tutor.py` `/ask` lane attaches the same payload.
- FE: `components/solve/TeachLoopCard.tsx` renders Understand/Check/Stretch/Next
  rail on the doubt page for all tiers; types in `solveTypes.ts`. Build clean,
  shipped on :3001.

### CISCE question bank — was EMPTY, now extracted
- `tools/extract_cisce_questions.py` (new) mines cisce_nodes_v2 (9,432 files) ->
  149,148 rows into `board_questions` tagged ICSE/ISC from the filename.
- `services/question_selector.py` + `practice_service.py`: `_resolve_board(student_id)`
  reads `User.board` (CISCE family -> ICSE for c<=10, ISC for 11-12); `board` param
  threads through `_corpus_candidates` / `get_next_question` / `select_practice_questions`
  / `_db_question_for_topic` / `_get_candidates`. Default stays "CBSE" — CISCE rows
  join the pool ONLY for CISCE students.

### Meta-leak sweep (authoring scratch in student-facing fields)
- mechanical pass: 200 files fixed (standalone "Revised/Better QUESTION:" labels,
  "Let's change the question..." sentences, "as an AI"/"I cannot assist").
- aggressive pass `_work/leak_fix3.py`: 433 files fixed — keeps post-"Revised" tail,
  drops draft steps superseded by "(Revised)" variants, truncates "Wait — actually"
  self-corrections; 82 unrecoverable sections deleted rather than served corrupted.
  Residual scan: 0 files leaking. 7 orphaned "- Step N:" labels dropped.
- false-positive guard: `{var}` inside backticks/f-strings/LaTeX braces is legal
  content (Python `{seat_no}`, `MU_{apple}`, `\begin{bmatrix}`) — excluded from fix.

### Assets verified
- node_sims.json: 60 dead `gen_*`/`d_*` refs removed (bind-mounted, live);
  3,637 sim ids -> 3,624 resolve via SIM_FILE_MAP (~99.6%; 13 gen_* ids absent by design).
- diagrams_index: 4,411/4,411 files present in public/diagrams.
- doodle_home_map: 10,925/10,925 nodes resolve to classN/<nid>.png.

### Ops
- Both backends restarted; index+embed caches cleared first so the fixed corpus
  rebuilds (same-count rewrites wouldn't trip FAST_FINGERPRINT otherwise).

## [OVERHAUL-WS-A-p4] 2026-10-10 — shared-state + path hardening (agent ovhA-p4, packet WSA-P4)

Claims:
- [fixed-ovhA-p4-memo-race] LANDED + gap COMPLETED by ovhA-p4r: `_ANS_MEMO_LOCK` (RLock) around load/get/put; OrderedDict LRU capped at ANSWER_MEMO_MAX_ROWS (default 5000; move_to_end touch on get; popitem(last=False) evict on put); merge-on-load AND merge-on-write (`_ans_memo_merge_file` re-reads disk under the lock before every write so a sibling uvicorn worker's rows are adopted, never clobbered); temp-file + os.replace UNDER the lock (no torn file, no dump iterating a mutating dict). Gap closed this session: ANSWER_MEMO_MAX_ROWS=0 was documented on _ans_memo_max_rows as the banking kill-switch but the code banked with the evictor off (unbounded) — put now returns before any write when cap<=0. Evidence: tests/test_wsa_shared_state.py test_memo_concurrent_puts (16 barrier threads, 16/16 rows survive disk reload), test_memo_worker_lost_update (2-worker clobber scenario merges), test_memo_cap (150 puts, cap 50 honored in memory+disk, newest survive), test_memo_get_is_version_checked_and_lru_touches, test_memo_cap_zero_disables_banking (red-first), test_memo_put_leaves_guided_out.
- [fixed-ovhA-p4-key-conn] LANDED by ovhA-p4r: per-call short-lived sqlite connection (no shared `_KEY_CONN` remains in code — only the historical docstring + a test's hasattr compat guard mention it; grep-proofed). MEASURED trade (native, 2026-10-10, 2000 lookups, bank on tmpfs-style local disk): per-call connect ~1.87ms vs locked shared conn ~0.17ms → ~1.7ms extra ≈ 0.13% of the ~1.3s answer p50; the simpler correct design wins — zero cross-thread state, no lock contention on the solve pool, and a rebuilt question_bank.db is picked up live (a locked shared conn would pin the old file handle). Evidence: tests/test_wsa_shared_state.py test_key_lookup_concurrent (8 threads x 25 = 200/200 hits under a barrier; the old check_same_thread=False shared conn lost up to 85% to InterfaceError storms), test_key_lookup_resolves_env_data_dir.
- [fixed-ovhA-p4-neuro-singleton] LANDED by ovhA-p4r (prior-state grep missed it because the marker reads "Double-checked locking (WSA-P4)" at neurolink.py:549-557): class-level `_instance_lock = threading.Lock()`, get_instance = unlocked fast path → lock → re-check → construct. Evidence: tests/test_wsa_shared_state.py test_neurolink_singleton_single_init (16 concurrent get_instance with a 30ms-widened __init__ window → exactly 1 construction, 1 unique instance id).
- [fixed-ovhA-p4-win-paths] LANDED by ovhA-p4r: new `its/solver/paths.py` (stdlib-only, no solver imports) — data_dir ladder: CBSE_DATA_DIR env AUTHORITATIVE (never falls through even if the dir is empty) → package-relative `<repo>/cbse_data` (the /app/cbse_data container mount) → legacy `D:\platform\runtime_data` literal LAST resort → package-relative writable default; reset_caches() for tests; env_file_candidates(): CBSE_ENV_FILE env → package-relative .env → legacy deploy .env literal. All three defect sites route through it: neurolink._read_db_password (POSTGRES_PASSWORD env first, then the env-file ladder — the hardcoded `D:\platform\deploy\.env` read silently killed DB-backed neurolink lanes in Linux), bank_answer._bank_npz_path (data_file("bank_q_embed.npz")), answer_pipeline._ans_memo_path + _key_bank_path (data_file). - [fixed-ovhA-p4-neuro-askgate] LANDED by ovhA-p4r: `_ask_gate(question, declared=())` — fail-open wrapper over ask_coherence.ask_coherent (any import/call failure returns True) — wired into ALL neurolink compute lanes (12 call sites incl. calculus, dimensional, graph, unit-consistency, fast_solve the v3 cascade funnel).
