Verified live: UI signup of `e2e2-student@atlas-test.com` (role=student, class 10) → **201 with zero parental consent recorded**. Source `deploy/backend/schemas/auth.py:37-50` REQUIRES both for student signups (422) and `deploy/frontend/app/(auth)/signup/page.tsx` collects them — so today's source is compliant but nothing of it is live. This is a stale-build artifact: the fix is already in the tree and ships when the coordinator ships the next build batch (ship-3); marked fixed-pending-ship, NOT a code defect. DPDP §9 gap remains open in production until ship-3 lands. SECOND-ORDER RISK: the shipped FE build and shipped backend are consistent-old; if EITHER side is rebuilt/redeployed alone, student signup either starts 422-ing (backend only) or keeps skipping consent (FE only). Repro: open /signup, count inputs; or `POST /api/auth/signup {"role":"student",...}` without parent fields → 201. Screenshots `_work/e2e2/shots/s1_*.png`.
- [fixed-e2e2-premiumgate] **MEDIUM — WS-PWA offline-fallback fix also unshipped — pending ship-3.** Same stale-build root cause: with the browser offline, navigating to `/learn` serves the OLD `/offline` PremiumGate card ("Offline mode — This is a Premium feature. Upgrade for ₹299/month") — the exact bug WS-PWA fixed today (minimal unauthenticated fallback; rich library moved to `/offline/library`). The fix is in the tree and ships when the coordinator ships ship-3; marked fixed-pending-ship, NOT a code defect. Evidence `s6_offline_nav.png`; running build predates the 2026-10-13 ships.
- [fixed-e2e2-anon] **MEDIUM — owned practice sessions accepted anonymous writes.** `POST /api/practice/{sid}/submit` returned 200 + grades after `localStorage.clear()` (zero credentials): `_enforce_session_owner` returned early on `caller_sub=None`, so anonymous callers always passed even on `auth_user_id`-stamped sessions — anyone holding a session URL could inject attempts into that student's mastery/streak. FIXED in `deploy/backend/api/practice.py::_enforce_session_owner` (~:186): an owned session now requires `str(owner) == str(caller_sub)` for ALL callers including anonymous (403 "This practice session belongs to another student."); ownerless/demo sessions keep capability-URL visibility. Pinned by `deploy/backend/tests/test_ovh_e2e2_session_owner.py` (8 tests: foreign caller 403, anonymous-on-owned 403, owner pass, string coercion, ownerless/demo unchanged). Original evidence `s5_after_expired_submit.png`, `_work/e2e2/report.md` §S5.
- [e2e2-create-slow] **LOW — teacher assignment create: >5s POST, no completion toast before card re-collapses.** In the browser run, clicking "Assign homework" left the card open ≥5s with the POST still in flight; the assignment DID land (row `a46174a8`) but the success toast/`load()` raced — the form looked like it did nothing. A teacher may click twice → duplicate assignments (the two identical "Quadratic equation in one variable x" rows now in the teacher list are mine — one from the slow UI create, one from a direct API probe). Mitigate: disable + persist "Creating…" until response, or toast on pending. Evidence `s3_created.png`.
- [e2e2-offline-418] **LOW — React #418 hydration errors on the offline fallback path.** 3× minified `Error #418` pageerrors while rendering the offline fallback after `setOffline` (route-level hydration mismatch). No crash; page still rendered. Evidence `s6_offline_nav.png` + pageerror log in report §S6.
- [e2e2-content-grammar] **LOW — board_questions stem served to students contains "Evaluate you's statement".** Served inside assignment `466a375a` (questionId `54c80d88-…`, c10 math quadratic set). Almost certainly "Evaluate your statement". Evidence `s3c_take` text capture in report §S3 + `GET /api/assignments/466a375a-…/result`.
- [e2e2-consent-copy] **INFO — once WS-LEGAL ships, the consent checkbox copy over-promises.** `signup/page.tsx` tells the parent "We'll notify them by email", but `services/mailer.py` only compose+logs (`consent_status='pending'`) until SMTP exists. DB state is honest; UI copy is not. Product decision: soften copy or accept the gap knowingly.
- [e2e2-soak-plateau] **RESOLVED — RSS plateaus ~8.5GB be1 / ~7.4GB be2 (drift −197/−274MB post-warmup); WS-F inconclusive leak verdict closed: no leak, high footprint noted.**

## [WAVE98-L1] 2026-10-13 — tiered response + /solve/check + surrender-first

- [wave98-L1-contract-a] Contract A shipped on `answer()` + `/api/practice/deterministic/*` payloads: `tier` (verified|bank_similar|method_only|guided), `trust{label,detail}`, `method{formula,steps,units,blanks}`, `similar{stem,solution_steps,answer,source}`, `check_endpoint`. **BREAKING-ISH:** the solve payload key `tier` previously carried the badge label — the badge now lives on `badge`; internal readers repointed. L2 consumes the new fields.
- [wave98-L1-solve-check] `POST /api/solve/check {question,answer}` -> `{ok,verdict:match|mismatch|unknown,hint}` via `model_problem()` + `independent_verifier.check_route()` (no new verifier); mismatch hint names the step to re-check with the true number's digit runs scrubbed; inherits the `/api/solve` 20/min expensive-path limiter; auth-optional.
- [wave98-L1-reveal] `POST /api/tutor/reveal` did not exist despite the brief — created on `api/solve_check.py` at the exact contract path; returns `best_available_payload` (bank_similar | method_only | guided), always `verified:false`. Engine-side: `_serve_reveal` now fires on the FIRST surrender plea with zero hints/attempts (rung precondition removed per contract) and attaches `tier_payload`; the non-reveal `expected_answer` leak guard is preserved.
- [wave98-L1-bank-similar] new `bank_answer.similar()` exemplar lane: trgm >= BANK_SIMILAR_MIN_SIMILARITY (0.55 default, clamped below the 0.93 serving floor), read-only, own 1.0s budget — NEVER a serving lane; its rows are worked exemplars only.
- [wave98-L1-replay-note] 200-stem replay: guided share 47.0% -> 44.0% (method_only absorbed 6 walls; bank_similar 0 on this prose-heavy sample). One apparent badge flip was a 60s budget timeout on a cold stem (memo invalidated by solver-version hash — by design); re-probe returned identical badge+answer.

### WAVE98-L1 rework (2026-10-13, review pass 2)

- [wave98-L1-answer_tier] Contract A field renamed `tier` -> `answer_tier`; `tier` restored to the badge label everywhere (live FE reads it pre-L2). `badge` kept as an alias on new payload paths. PipelineResult carries `answer_tier`, never `tier`.
- [fixed-wave98-L1-similar-lane] `bank_answer.similar()` fired 0/200 because `pg_trgm.similarity_threshold=0.30` exploded the `%` prefilter past the 1.5s statement_timeout — even exact `norm_text =` rows missed. Now exact-equality first (ORDER BY id), trgm fallback with operator threshold = floor (0.55). Warm probe: 26/29 guided CBSE ids now return exemplars.
- [wave98-L1-bank-miss-rootcause] 29 guided CBSE replay rows tabulated: 16 were cold-cache `over_budget` flakes (warm lookup hits VERIFIED_OFFICIAL), 6 `ambiguous_template_text` (correct refusal, exemplar now served), 3 over_budget on NULL `norm_text` (L3 backfill), 2 flagged (correct exclusion), 1 null-norm. Serving budget + ambiguity gates unchanged — zero-wrong holds.
- [wave98-L1-replay-v2] guided share 47% -> 31% (62 left = 44 ICSE + 15 ISC null-norm, 3 CBSE); 0 badge diffs, 0 answer diffs.

### WAVE98-L1 rework (2026-10-14, review pass 3 — L2-flagged MEDIUM leak, FIXED)

- [fixed-wave98-L1-legacy-steps-leak] **FIXED the L2-flagged MEDIUM item** (`wave98-L2-bug-method-steps-leak`, confirmed live on dev :8030 with a `bank_similar` payload leaking "5/12" in top-level `steps` incl. a "Verification: ... reproduces 5/12" line, plus `socratic.board_steps` ending "∴ 5/12"). `tiered_response.stamp_tier` now scrubs the legacy student-facing fields on EVERY non-verified tier: `steps` + `socratic.board_steps` get the same `____` blanking as `method.steps` (banned = the computed answer's surface forms — whole strings + numeric renderings + the bare run for single-run answers; composite answers like "5/12" ban only the whole token + spacing variants so stated givens survive), `answer`/`numeric` are forced to None, and stray `working`/`solution`/`explanation` fields are scrubbed or dropped when unblankable. `stamp_tier` gained `question=` — when `model` is absent (bank_similar stamps never ran the sweep) one memo-warm `model_problem(question)` derives the banned set; `answer()` passes `question=text`. `verified` untouched; badge/verdict logic unchanged. Tests: `its/tests/test_wave98_tiered.py::TestLegacyFieldsScrubbed` (7 new); `test_wave98_tiered.py + test_solve_isolation.py` → 35 passed (`_work/wave98/test_run_leakfix.txt`). Live-verified on dev :8030 post-fix (`_work/wave98/repro_after*.json`): bank_similar ships `answer:null` + blanked steps; method_only blanks final `5/33` while keeping legit intermediates. Residual: only literal surface forms are banned — an unreduced equivalent intermediate ("20/132") can remain as working; same limit as the `method.steps` scrub.

## [WAVE98-L3] 2026-10-13 — pre-route + pre-warm + soft budget + norm_text backfill

Evidence: `_work/wave98/L3_report.md`; tests `tests/test_norm_backfill.py` + `tests/test_prewarm.py` (new) + `tests/test_solve_isolation.py` + `tests/test_worker_jobs.py` → 49 passed. Artifacts: `backfill_dryrun.json`, `preroute_2k.log`, `latency_cold.json`/`latency_warm.json`, `test_run_l3.txt`.

- [added-wave98-L3-backfill] `workers/norm_backfill.py` (registered `norm_text_backfill`, HEAVY window): spec-verbatim chunked backfill — 5k id-ordered chunks, per-row `AND norm_text IS NULL` idempotent re-check, resumable cursor `norm_backfill.cursor` under the runtime-data mount, 0.5s sleep + 1.5GB RSS tripwire, dry-run mode, post-run ANALYZE + trigram-index REPORT (no DDL creation). Dry-run on prod: CBSE 1,468 / ICSE 59,309 / ISC 89,839 NULLs; `idx_bq_norm_trgm` already present.
- [added-wave98-L3-preroute] `workers/preroute.py` (registered `bank_preroute`, HEAVY window): route-only `model_problem()` sweep over norm_text-populated rows; `SOLVER_LLM_MODE=off` hard assertion; 2k batches, cursor `bank_preroute.cursor`. Pilot: 2,000 rows / 901s (~2.2 rows/s), routed 102, RSS tripped 1.5GB once and paused correctly. **Full bank ≈ 31h ≈ ~10 heavy-window nights** — coordinator should expect multi-night completion (cursor resumes across windows; the pilot's cursor already lives on the prod-mounted runtime_data volume and will carry over).
- [added-wave98-L3-prewarm] `services/prewarm.py` + hook in `routers/content.py` chapter open: fire-and-forget `session_prewarm` enqueue on the postgres job queue (no Redis in this stack), 1h per-process dedupe, `prewarm-<node>` deterministic job id; every failure swallowed. Worker handler runs top-50 route+bank lookups for the node.
- [added-wave98-L3-softbudget] `api/verified_practice.py`: `SOLVE_SOFT_BUDGET_S` (default 6s, capped by SOLVE_BUDGET_S) bounds the deterministic-solve await; past it the endpoint serves the best-available Contract A payload (`upgrading: true`, `stage: soft_budget`, answer withheld) while the compute finishes on the pool into cache — retry is instant (`solve:background_cache`). Verified serving-side: fresh stem → soft payload at ~6.3s wall; retry → `cached:true` at 456ms.
- [fixed-wave98-L3-dev-llm] **dev :8030 drift from prod posture:** `SOLVER_LLM_MODE` unset → config default `parse_only` → with real GROQ/GEMINI keys in `deploy/.env`, `structure_extractor._chat` made REAL outbound LLM calls during dev solves (groq 413s/200s, gemini 503/50s read timeouts, localhost:3001 404) — serving walls inflated to 27-47s and background solves burned quota. Prod runs `off` (unaffected); dev verify boots must pin `SOLVER_LLM_MODE=off SOLVER_OFFLINE=1`. The verify instance now runs with them.
- [wave98-L3-softbudget-tail] soft-budget serving wall is ~6.0s (metrics `latency_ms`≈6005) + ~0.5-2s best-available build — the 1.0s `wait_for` bound around `to_thread` is not a hard preempt, and under pool contention observed walls reached ~12-16s on the dev box. Inside L2's ">6s then upgrades" tolerance; flagged for a prod eyeball post-ship.

- [added-wave98-L2-router] `components/answer/TierCard.tsx` — single Contract A router keyed on `answer_tier`: verified → VerifiedAnswerCard; bank_similar → SimilarBoardQuestion; method_only → MethodSkeleton; guided/legacy → the existing honest guided sheet. `tier`/`badge` semantics untouched; `answerTierOf` falls back conservatively (legacy non-verified → guided view, never upgrades a badge).
- [added-wave98-L2-trust] `components/answer/TrustLabel.tsx` — one-line `{label}` chip + `trust.detail` verbatim; tier color (verified green / bank_similar+method_only amber / guided neutral) PLUS icon and text so status never rests on color alone; `tone="dark"` variant for Atlas-glass surfaces (/solve-page rows). Mounted on every solve surface that carries `trust` (doubt single + multi verdicts, practice solver, voice solve, whole-page solve, reveal payload).
- [added-wave98-L2-method] `components/answer/MethodSkeleton.tsx` — formula bar, numbered blanked steps (____ rendered as dashed fill-slots with sr-only cue), units line, labelled "Check my answer" input → POST `{question, answer}` to `check_endpoint`; match shows green tick + API hint, mismatch shows hint verbatim (never a number), unknown shows the contract line; loading/disabled/aria-live states; 44px touch targets, focus-visible outline. Renders ONLY `method.steps` — see leak bug below. `MethodSkeletonPlaceholder` ghost (spinner + "Working through it…" + method-box ghost) exported for submit-instant perceived latency.
- [added-wave98-L2-similar] `components/answer/SimilarBoardQuestion.tsx` — "Same type from a board paper" header, exemplar stem/steps, exemplar answer in a dashed box labelled "That question's answer:", `Source:` footer, divider, "Now try yours — same method" echoing the still-unsolved student stem. Bank answer is never presented as the student's answer.
- [added-wave98-L2-surrender] "Just show me — I'm out of time" rides first-screen on every guided + method_only card (44px, not behind hints): POSTs `/api/tutor/reveal` `{question, board, class_level, subject}`, renders the returned payload re-tiered by ITS `answer_tier`/`trust` (always `verified:false` — a reveal can never stamp verified); error state offers retry, loading announced via role=status.
- [added-wave98-L2-upgrade] `upgrading:true` soft-budget handling on doubt + practice: provisional card renders immediately, one silent re-poll of the SAME endpoint/payload after ~1.5s swaps the card in place only when the upgrade landed (`!upgrading`); timers cleared on new submit/reset/reopen so a stale re-poll can never clobber a newer question's card; "still verifying" note via aria-live.
- [fixed-wave98-L2-copy] Contract tone applied on all touched surfaces: friendlyTier maps GUIDED_* → "Let's build it together" (solveTypes + both page-local copies); "cannot/can't verify" → "could not verify"; "Recheck failed" → "The re-check did not go through"; AskResponseCard "Guided" badge → "Let's build it together" and "a wrong shortcut sticks" → "a quick guess sticks". Internal identifiers (`refusal_reason`, state names) left as-is — backend field names, not display text.
- [wave98-L2-bug-method-steps-leak] **MEDIUM — backend contract gap (L1 surface):** ~~`method_only` payloads blank the answer inside `method.steps` but the legacy top-level `steps` array still ships the unblanked computed text~~ **FIXED 2026-10-14 in L1 rework pass 3 — see `[fixed-wave98-L1-legacy-steps-leak]` under [WAVE98-L1].** Original finding: (live probe: `"triangle angle sum: 180 - 30 - 80 = 70 deg."` alongside `method.steps: "... = ____."`). L2 renderers use `method.steps` exclusively so nothing leaks on doubt/practice/voice, but ANY other/future renderer of `payload.steps` on a method_only payload leaks the number — zero-wrong-adjacent. Owner fix: blank top-level `steps` too when `answer_tier === 'method_only'`, or drop it from that tier's payload.
- [wave98-L2-check-coverage] `/api/solve/check` returns `unknown` for similarity-style stems (no check_route) — UI renders all three verdict branches; observed live "I can't check this one — but the method above is still right." on the triangle stem. Honest, but check coverage is narrow on prose/geometry lanes; flagged for L1 follow-up if check coverage is meant to widen.
- [wave98-L2-verify-notes] verify build proxies `/api/*` via next.config `INTERNAL_API_URL` — BAKED INTO THE BUILD at build time (routes-manifest), so `next start` cannot be re-pointed by env alone; screenshots therefore ran on a `next dev` instance (`INTERNAL_API_URL=http://localhost:8030`, `.next-verify-dev` dist dir) — same code path, live compilation. Prod build verified clean via `.next-verify` (baked :8001 = correct for prod docker backend which will carry the wave98 routes post-ship).

Evidence: `_work/wave98/L4_report.md`; tests `its/tests/test_wave98_l4_prose_lang.py` (30) + tiered + tutor regression → 68 passed; eval `_work/wave98/l4_eval.py` → 120/120 within ±1 (`l4_eval_results.json`); live curls `l4_curl_*.json` on dev :8030.

- [added-wave98-L4-prose-grade] `services/marking_scheme_grader.py` + `routers/grade_prose.py` (`POST /api/prose/grade`, auth-optional): deterministic point-coverage grading against `board_questions.marking_scheme` (167k rows, ~41% populated — primary source; `model_answer` numbered items / node `marking_points` + competency STEPS resolve ahead/fallback). Scheme-glue tokens (df≥50% across a scheme) carry zero hit weight; `Generalize/Extend` steps excluded; duplicate key-set points collapsed. Feedback quotes the strongest missed point verbatim. No marking data → honest `verdict:"unknown"`, never a fabricated score.

- [added-wave98-L4-lang] tutor session `language` (`en|hi|hinglish`) — `StudentState.language` persists via snapshot round-trip; settable on `its/start`, `its/input`, and WS `its_start`/`its_input`; all engine fixed utterances route through `its/tutoring/utterances.py`; authored Devanagari hi templates pass a no-stray-ASCII test (allowlist = literal command keywords only); student text/node content never translated; `en` byte-identical.
- [wave98-L4-gradable-coverage] flag is correct-but-rare on the dev corpus: bank-matched prose stems land `verified`/`bank_similar` before `guided`, and all answerless scheme rows are flagged-out — gradable fires on node-context guided stems + residual marking-resolution gaps. Ratchet widening (e.g. probing bank_similar too) needs a product call — a weaker floor would grade against the wrong scheme.
- [wave98-L4-normtext-stale] found live: some `board_questions.norm_text` values are stale vs `question_text` (observed sim ~0.54) — those rows honestly miss norm/trgm marking resolution; L3 backfill owns freshness.

### FAIL — [REGRESSION] story_url / storyUrl node-id stripped by the display boundary -> dead doubt->story and chapter-test "Story" links

- **What:** Every TEACH/NEAR_MISS response from `POST /api/tutor-graph/ask` carries `story_url: "/learn//story"` — the node id segment is empty. Chapter-test result `nodeBreakdown[].storyUrl` is identically mangled. The frontend `AskResponseCard`/`MediaBridges` renders "Open the full Story" with `href="/learn//story"` -> 308 -> `/learn/story` (200 but a wrong dead destination — no node content). `node_id` itself arrives intact (`c10_math_icse_15_008`) because `node_id` IS whitelisted.
- **Root cause:** `deploy/backend/services/display_scrub.py` `_FETCH_KEY_FIELDS_RAW` (lines 578-594) whitelists `url`, `uri`, `href`, `link`, `image_url`, `avatar_url`, `audio_url`, `video_url`, `icon_url`, `doodle_url` — but NOT `story_url`/`storyUrl` (normalizes to `storyurl`, absent from the set). So `clean_student_payload` runs `student_clean` over the URL and `_ID_TOKEN`/`_ID_TOKEN_LOOSE` strip the `c10_math_cbse_10_001` segment. Boundary installed 2026-10-04 (`main.py:512-513` `install_display_boundary(app)`) — after the 09-25 sweep which verified live `story_url`. REGRESSION window: post-10-04.
- **Repro (30s):** `curl -X POST http://localhost:3001/api/tutor-graph/ask -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -H 'Authorization: Bearer <student token>' -d '{"student_id":"<uid>","question":"Why is a tangent perpendicular to the radius?","class_level":10}'` -> `{"story_url":"/learn//story","node_id":"c10_math_*_*_*"}` (200, ~220ms). UI: open any `/learn/<id>/story` -> "Got a doubt?" -> ask -> answer card renders "Open the full Story" linking to `/learn//story` (screenshot 07_doubt_answer.png; DOM proof `a[data-testid="story-link"] href="/learn//story"`).
- **Fix (not applied — verify-only):** add `story_url storyurl story_link` (or a `*_url`-suffix rule) to `_FETCH_KEY_FIELDS_RAW`. One-line whitelist entry; node ids in URLs are already owner-sanctioned fetch keys (2026-09-29 law).
- **Blast radius:** doubt->story bridge (StoryMode + atlas page + doubt panels), chapter-test weak-node Story links, any other response field literally named `story_url`/`storyUrl`. `link`/`href`-named fields are safe (whitelisted).

### Anomalies observed (not failures)

- One transient 404 on owner-A `/api/practice/{sid}/submit` mid-run (non-reproducible; 3x 200 on identical repro — suspected cross-replica session-visibility blip under nginx round-robin; watch under load).
- `/api/tutor/ask` (dashboard ask box) hit its designed 10s budget twice under cold/concurrent load -> honest timeout fallback text; warm responses are 107-1267ms `deterministic_zenith`. Borderline acceptable; revisit if recurrent.
- Rate limiter (20/min expensive) will 429 a sub-second 15-answer diagnostic storm — by design, human pace unaffected.
